@err_067	--hel
status 0
o:Usage: diff [OPTION]... FILES
o:Compare FILES line by line.
o:
o:Mandatory arguments to long options are mandatory for short options too.
o:\x20     --normal                  output a normal diff (the default)
o:\x20 -q, --brief                   report only when files differ
o:\x20 -s, --report-identical-files  report when two files are the same
o:\x20 -c, -C NUM, --context[=NUM]   output NUM (default 3) lines of copied context
o:\x20 -u, -U NUM, --unified[=NUM]   output NUM (default 3) lines of unified context
o:\x20 -e, --ed                      output an ed script
o:\x20 -n, --rcs                     output an RCS format diff
o:\x20 -y, --side-by-side            output in two columns
o:\x20 -W, --width=NUM               output at most NUM (default 130) print columns
o:\x20     --left-column             output only the left column of common lines
o:\x20     --suppress-common-lines   do not output common lines
o:
o:\x20 -p, --show-c-function         show which C function each change is in
o:\x20 -F, --show-function-line=RE   show the most recent line matching RE
o:\x20     --label LABEL             use LABEL instead of file name and timestamp
o:\x20                                 (can be repeated)
o:
o:\x20 -t, --expand-tabs             expand tabs to spaces in output
o:\x20 -T, --initial-tab             make tabs line up by prepending a tab
o:\x20     --tabsize=NUM             tab stops every NUM (default 8) print columns
o:\x20     --suppress-blank-empty    suppress space or tab before empty output lines
o:\x20 -l, --paginate                pass output through 'pr' to paginate it
o:
o:\x20 -r, --recursive                 recursively compare any subdirectories found
o:\x20     --no-dereference            don't follow symbolic links
o:\x20 -N, --new-file                  treat absent files as empty
o:\x20     --unidirectional-new-file   treat absent first files as empty
o:\x20     --ignore-file-name-case     ignore case when comparing file names
o:\x20     --no-ignore-file-name-case  consider case when comparing file names
o:\x20 -x, --exclude=PAT               exclude files that match PAT
o:\x20 -X, --exclude-from=FILE         exclude files that match any pattern in FILE
o:\x20 -S, --starting-file=FILE        start with FILE when comparing directories
o:\x20     --from-file=FILE1           compare FILE1 to all operands;
o:\x20                                   FILE1 can be a directory
o:\x20     --to-file=FILE2             compare all operands to FILE2;
o:\x20                                   FILE2 can be a directory
o:
o:\x20 -i, --ignore-case               ignore case differences in file contents
o:\x20 -E, --ignore-tab-expansion      ignore changes due to tab expansion
o:\x20 -Z, --ignore-trailing-space     ignore white space at line end
o:\x20 -b, --ignore-space-change       ignore changes in the amount of white space
o:\x20 -w, --ignore-all-space          ignore all white space
o:\x20 -B, --ignore-blank-lines        ignore changes where lines are all blank
o:\x20 -I, --ignore-matching-lines=RE  ignore changes where all lines match RE
o:
o:\x20 -a, --text                      treat all files as text
o:\x20     --strip-trailing-cr         strip trailing carriage return on input
o:
o:\x20 -D, --ifdef=NAME                output merged file with '#ifdef NAME' diffs
o:\x20     --GTYPE-group-format=GFMT   format GTYPE input groups with GFMT
o:\x20     --line-format=LFMT          format all input lines with LFMT
o:\x20     --LTYPE-line-format=LFMT    format LTYPE input lines with LFMT
o:\x20   These format options provide fine-grained control over the output
o:\x20     of diff, generalizing -D/--ifdef.
o:\x20   LTYPE is 'old', 'new', or 'unchanged'.  GTYPE is LTYPE or 'changed'.
o:\x20   GFMT (only) may contain:
o:\x20     %<  lines from FILE1
o:\x20     %>  lines from FILE2
o:\x20     %=  lines common to FILE1 and FILE2
o:\x20     %[-][WIDTH][.[PREC]]{doxX}LETTER  printf-style spec for LETTER
o:\x20       LETTERs are as follows for new group, lower case for old group:
o:\x20         F  first line number
o:\x20         L  last line number
o:\x20         N  number of lines = L-F+1
o:\x20         E  F-1
o:\x20         M  L+1
o:\x20     %(A=B?T:E)  if A equals B then T else E
o:\x20   LFMT (only) may contain:
o:\x20     %L  contents of line
o:\x20     %l  contents of line, excluding any trailing newline
o:\x20     %[-][WIDTH][.[PREC]]{doxX}n  printf-style spec for input line number
o:\x20   Both GFMT and LFMT may contain:
o:\x20     %%  %
o:\x20     %c'C'  the single character C
o:\x20     %c'\\OOO'  the character with octal code OOO
o:\x20     C    the character C (other characters represent themselves)
o:
o:\x20 -d, --minimal            try hard to find a smaller set of changes
o:\x20     --horizon-lines=NUM  keep NUM lines of the common prefix and suffix
o:\x20     --speed-large-files  assume large files and many scattered small changes
o:\x20     --color[=WHEN]       color output; WHEN is 'never', 'always', or 'auto';
o:\x20                            plain --color means --color='auto'
o:\x20     --palette=PALETTE    the colors to use when --color is active; PALETTE is
o:\x20                            a colon-separated list of terminfo capabilities
o:
o:\x20     --help               display this help and exit
o:\x20 -v, --version            output version information and exit
o:
o:FILES are 'FILE1 FILE2' or 'DIR1 DIR2' or 'DIR FILE' or 'FILE DIR'.
o:If --from-file or --to-file is given, there are no restrictions on FILE(s).
o:If a FILE is '-', read standard input.
o:Exit status is 0 if inputs are the same, 1 if different, 2 if trouble.
o:
o:Report bugs to: bug-diffutils@gnu.org
o:GNU diffutils home page: <https://www.gnu.org/software/diffutils/>
o:General help using GNU software: <https://www.gnu.org/gethelp/>

@err_065	--help
status 0
o:Usage: diff [OPTION]... FILES
o:Compare FILES line by line.
o:
o:Mandatory arguments to long options are mandatory for short options too.
o:\x20     --normal                  output a normal diff (the default)
o:\x20 -q, --brief                   report only when files differ
o:\x20 -s, --report-identical-files  report when two files are the same
o:\x20 -c, -C NUM, --context[=NUM]   output NUM (default 3) lines of copied context
o:\x20 -u, -U NUM, --unified[=NUM]   output NUM (default 3) lines of unified context
o:\x20 -e, --ed                      output an ed script
o:\x20 -n, --rcs                     output an RCS format diff
o:\x20 -y, --side-by-side            output in two columns
o:\x20 -W, --width=NUM               output at most NUM (default 130) print columns
o:\x20     --left-column             output only the left column of common lines
o:\x20     --suppress-common-lines   do not output common lines
o:
o:\x20 -p, --show-c-function         show which C function each change is in
o:\x20 -F, --show-function-line=RE   show the most recent line matching RE
o:\x20     --label LABEL             use LABEL instead of file name and timestamp
o:\x20                                 (can be repeated)
o:
o:\x20 -t, --expand-tabs             expand tabs to spaces in output
o:\x20 -T, --initial-tab             make tabs line up by prepending a tab
o:\x20     --tabsize=NUM             tab stops every NUM (default 8) print columns
o:\x20     --suppress-blank-empty    suppress space or tab before empty output lines
o:\x20 -l, --paginate                pass output through 'pr' to paginate it
o:
o:\x20 -r, --recursive                 recursively compare any subdirectories found
o:\x20     --no-dereference            don't follow symbolic links
o:\x20 -N, --new-file                  treat absent files as empty
o:\x20     --unidirectional-new-file   treat absent first files as empty
o:\x20     --ignore-file-name-case     ignore case when comparing file names
o:\x20     --no-ignore-file-name-case  consider case when comparing file names
o:\x20 -x, --exclude=PAT               exclude files that match PAT
o:\x20 -X, --exclude-from=FILE         exclude files that match any pattern in FILE
o:\x20 -S, --starting-file=FILE        start with FILE when comparing directories
o:\x20     --from-file=FILE1           compare FILE1 to all operands;
o:\x20                                   FILE1 can be a directory
o:\x20     --to-file=FILE2             compare all operands to FILE2;
o:\x20                                   FILE2 can be a directory
o:
o:\x20 -i, --ignore-case               ignore case differences in file contents
o:\x20 -E, --ignore-tab-expansion      ignore changes due to tab expansion
o:\x20 -Z, --ignore-trailing-space     ignore white space at line end
o:\x20 -b, --ignore-space-change       ignore changes in the amount of white space
o:\x20 -w, --ignore-all-space          ignore all white space
o:\x20 -B, --ignore-blank-lines        ignore changes where lines are all blank
o:\x20 -I, --ignore-matching-lines=RE  ignore changes where all lines match RE
o:
o:\x20 -a, --text                      treat all files as text
o:\x20     --strip-trailing-cr         strip trailing carriage return on input
o:
o:\x20 -D, --ifdef=NAME                output merged file with '#ifdef NAME' diffs
o:\x20     --GTYPE-group-format=GFMT   format GTYPE input groups with GFMT
o:\x20     --line-format=LFMT          format all input lines with LFMT
o:\x20     --LTYPE-line-format=LFMT    format LTYPE input lines with LFMT
o:\x20   These format options provide fine-grained control over the output
o:\x20     of diff, generalizing -D/--ifdef.
o:\x20   LTYPE is 'old', 'new', or 'unchanged'.  GTYPE is LTYPE or 'changed'.
o:\x20   GFMT (only) may contain:
o:\x20     %<  lines from FILE1
o:\x20     %>  lines from FILE2
o:\x20     %=  lines common to FILE1 and FILE2
o:\x20     %[-][WIDTH][.[PREC]]{doxX}LETTER  printf-style spec for LETTER
o:\x20       LETTERs are as follows for new group, lower case for old group:
o:\x20         F  first line number
o:\x20         L  last line number
o:\x20         N  number of lines = L-F+1
o:\x20         E  F-1
o:\x20         M  L+1
o:\x20     %(A=B?T:E)  if A equals B then T else E
o:\x20   LFMT (only) may contain:
o:\x20     %L  contents of line
o:\x20     %l  contents of line, excluding any trailing newline
o:\x20     %[-][WIDTH][.[PREC]]{doxX}n  printf-style spec for input line number
o:\x20   Both GFMT and LFMT may contain:
o:\x20     %%  %
o:\x20     %c'C'  the single character C
o:\x20     %c'\\OOO'  the character with octal code OOO
o:\x20     C    the character C (other characters represent themselves)
o:
o:\x20 -d, --minimal            try hard to find a smaller set of changes
o:\x20     --horizon-lines=NUM  keep NUM lines of the common prefix and suffix
o:\x20     --speed-large-files  assume large files and many scattered small changes
o:\x20     --color[=WHEN]       color output; WHEN is 'never', 'always', or 'auto';
o:\x20                            plain --color means --color='auto'
o:\x20     --palette=PALETTE    the colors to use when --color is active; PALETTE is
o:\x20                            a colon-separated list of terminfo capabilities
o:
o:\x20     --help               display this help and exit
o:\x20 -v, --version            output version information and exit
o:
o:FILES are 'FILE1 FILE2' or 'DIR1 DIR2' or 'DIR FILE' or 'FILE DIR'.
o:If --from-file or --to-file is given, there are no restrictions on FILE(s).
o:If a FILE is '-', read standard input.
o:Exit status is 0 if inputs are the same, 1 if different, 2 if trouble.
o:
o:Report bugs to: bug-diffutils@gnu.org
o:GNU diffutils home page: <https://www.gnu.org/software/diffutils/>
o:General help using GNU software: <https://www.gnu.org/gethelp/>
